(herald "Verification of TLS 1.3 rules to assist in mTLS verification"
  (try-old-strands))

(comment "CPSA 4.4.9")
(comment "All input read from reference_m1.scm")
(comment "Strand count bounded at 30")
(comment "Old strands tried first")

(defprotocol mtls13 basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
              (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
                (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
              (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
                (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defrule clients-servers-distinct
    (forall ((z0 z1 strd) (c name))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" "s" z1 c) (p "client" "c" z0 c))
        (false))))
  (defrule client-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((x rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 1)) (p "" "x" z-0 x)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule server-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((y rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 2)) (p "" "x" z-0 y)
          (p "server" "y" z y) (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defrule diffie-hellman-value-between-legitimate-parties-unavailable-1
    (forall
      ((cr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 2))
          (p "client" "spk" z spk) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "" "x" z-0 (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
          (p "client" "y" z y) (p "client" "x" z x)
          (p "client" "cr" z cr) (non (invk spk)) (uniq-at cr z (idx 0))
          (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule diffie-hellman-value-between-legitimate-parties-unavailable-2
    (forall
      ((sr random32) (cpk akey) (c ca name) (y rndx) (x expt)
        (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 3))
          (p "server" "cpk" z cpk) (p "server" "ca" z ca)
          (p "server" "c" z c) (p "" "x" z-0 (exp (gen) (mul y x)))
          (p "server" "y" z y) (p "server" "x" z x)
          (p "server" "sr" z sr) (non (invk cpk)) (uniq-at sr z (idx 1))
          (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

(defskeleton mtls13
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (spk cpk akey)
    (s c ca name) (x rndx) (y expt))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
    (y y))
  (non-orig (invk spk))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x)
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk cpk akey) (s c ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "client" "c" z c) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y) (uniq-at cr z 0)
          (ugen x) (non (invk spk)))
        (exists ((z-0 strd))
          (and (p "server" z-0 5) (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr)
            (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "server" "spk" z-0 spk)
            (p "server" "s" z-0 s) (p "server" "cpk" z-0 cpk)
            (p "server" "c" z-0 c) (p "server" "ca" z-0 ca)
            (p "server" "x" z-0 x) (p "server" "y" z-0 y))))))
  (traces
    ((send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
              (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
                (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))))
  (label 0)
  (realized)
  (shape)
  (satisfies
    (no (p "server" z-0 (idx 5)) (p "server" "y" z-0 y)
      (p "server" "x" z-0 x) (p "server" "ca" z-0 ca)
      (p "server" "c" z-0 c) (p "server" "cpk" z-0 cpk)
      (p "server" "s" z-0 s) (p "server" "spk" z-0 spk)
      (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr) (cr cr) (sr sr)
      (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x) (y y) (z 0)))
  (maps
    ((0)
      ((cr cr) (sr sr) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
        (y y) (request request) (response response))))
  (origs (cr (0 0)))
  (ugens (x (0 0))))

(comment "Nothing left to do")

(defskeleton mtls13
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (spk cpk akey)
    (s c ca name) (x expt) (y rndx))
  (defstrand server 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (y y)
    (x x))

  (uniq-orig sr)
  (uniq-gen y)
  (absent (y x))

    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk cpk akey) (s c ca name) (x expt) (y rndx)

          (ugen y) (non (invk cpk)))
        (exists ((z-0 strd))
          (and (p "client" z-0 4) (p "client" "cr" z-0 cr)
            (p "client" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "client" "spk" z-0 spk)
            (p "client" "s" z-0 s) (p "client" "cpk" z-0 cpk)

              (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))

              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk

        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))))
  (label 1)
  (unrealized (0 2))

    ((2 1) (0 3)))

      (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr

(defprotocol mtls13 basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
              (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
                (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
              (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
                (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defrule clients-servers-distinct
    (forall ((z0 z1 strd) (c name))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" "s" z1 c) (p "client" "c" z0 c))
        (false))))
  (defrule client-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((x rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 1)) (p "" "x" z-0 x)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule server-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((y rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 2)) (p "" "x" z-0 y)
          (p "server" "y" z y) (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defrule diffie-hellman-value-between-legitimate-parties-unavailable-1
    (forall
      ((cr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 2))
          (p "client" "spk" z spk) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "" "x" z-0 (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
          (p "client" "y" z y) (p "client" "x" z x)
          (p "client" "cr" z cr) (non (invk spk)) (uniq-at cr z (idx 0))
          (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule diffie-hellman-value-between-legitimate-parties-unavailable-2
    (forall
      ((sr random32) (cpk akey) (c ca name) (y rndx) (x expt)
        (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 3))
          (p "server" "cpk" z cpk) (p "server" "ca" z ca)
          (p "server" "c" z c) (p "" "x" z-0 (exp (gen) (mul y x)))
          (p "server" "y" z y) (p "server" "x" z x)
          (p "server" "sr" z sr) (non (invk cpk)) (uniq-at sr z (idx 1))
          (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

          (uniq-at request z 3)) (false))))

              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))

  (defrule diffie-hellman-value-between-legitimate-parties-unavailable-1
    (forall
      ((cr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 2))
          (p "client" "spk" z spk) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "" "x" z-0 (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
          (p "client" "y" z y) (p "client" "x" z x)
          (p "client" "cr" z cr) (non (invk spk)) (uniq-at cr z (idx 0))
          (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule diffie-hellman-value-between-legitimate-parties-unavailable-2
    (forall
      ((sr random32) (cpk akey) (c ca name) (y rndx) (x expt)
        (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 3))
          (p "server" "cpk" z cpk) (p "server" "ca" z ca)
          (p "server" "c" z c) (p "" "x" z-0 (exp (gen) (mul y x)))
          (p "server" "y" z y) (p "server" "x" z x)
          (p "server" "sr" z sr) (non (invk cpk)) (uniq-at sr z (idx 1))
          (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

(defprotocol mtls13 basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
              (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
                (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))

      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))

              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))

                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
              (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
              (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) c cpk
                (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))

          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defrule clients-servers-distinct
    (forall ((z0 z1 strd) (c name))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" "s" z1 c) (p "client" "c" z0 c))
        (false))))
  (defrule client-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((x rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 1)) (p "" "x" z-0 x)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule server-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((y rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 2)) (p "" "x" z-0 y)
          (p "server" "y" z y) (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defrule diffie-hellman-value-between-legitimate-parties-unavailable-1
    (forall
      ((cr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 2))
          (p "client" "spk" z spk) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "" "x" z-0 (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
          (p "client" "y" z y) (p "client" "x" z x)
          (p "client" "cr" z cr) (non (invk spk)) (uniq-at cr z (idx 0))
          (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule diffie-hellman-value-between-legitimate-parties-unavailable-2
    (forall
      ((sr random32) (cpk akey) (c ca name) (y rndx) (x expt)
        (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 3))
          (p "server" "cpk" z cpk) (p "server" "ca" z ca)
          (p "server" "c" z c) (p "" "x" z-0 (exp (gen) (mul y x)))
          (p "server" "y" z y) (p "server" "x" z x)
          (p "server" "sr" z sr) (non (invk cpk)) (uniq-at sr z (idx 1))
          (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))
